 | Escape   | `ClearSelection`        |\n198: | No selection         | Escape   | `EscapeSmartClear`      |\n199: \n200: ---\n201: \n202: ## Context Conditions\n203: \n204: Some bindings only activate in specific contexts. Use the `when` field to specify conditions:\n205: \n206: | Condition              | Description                              |\n207: |------------------------|------------------------------------------|\n208: | `has_selection`        | At least one cursor has a selection      |\n209: | `no_selection`         | No cursors have selections               |\n210: | `has_multiple_cursors` | More than one cursor is active           |\n211: | `single_cursor`        | Exactly one cursor is active             |\n212: | `modal_active`         | A modal dialog is open                   |\n213: \n214: Example:\n215: ```yaml\n216: - key: \"tab\"\n217:   command: IndentLines\n218:   when: [\"has_selection\"]\n219: ```\n220: \n221: ---\n222: \n223: ## Custom Keybindings\n224: \n225: To customize keybindings, create `~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml`:\n226: \n227: ```yaml\n228: bindings:\n229:   # Override an existing binding\n230:   - key: \"cmd+s\"\n231:     command: SaveFileAs  # Make Cmd+S always \"Save As\"\n232: \n233:   # Add a new binding\n234:   - key: \"cmd+shift+d\"\n235:     command: DeleteLine\n236: \n237:   # Context-aware binding\n238:   - key: \"cmd+enter\"\n239:     command: InsertNewlineBelow\n240:     when: [\"no_selection\"]\n241: ```\n242: \n243: User bindings are merged with defaults. User bindings take precedence over defaults when keys match.\n244: \n245: ---\n246: \n247: ## Disabling Default Bindings\n248: \n249: To disable a default binding, use the `Unbound` command:\n250: \n251: ```yaml\n252: bindings:\n253:   # Disable Cmd+D (Duplicate)\n254:   - key: \"cmd+d\"\n255:     command: Unbound\n256: ```\n257: \n258: ---\n259: \n260: ## Key Names Reference\n261: \n262: ### Letters and Numbers\n263: `a` through `z`, `0` through `9`\n264: \n265: ### Named Keys\n266: `enter`, `escape`, `tab`, `backspace`, `delete`, `space`, `insert`\n267: \n268: ### Arrow Keys\n269: `up`, `down`, `left`, `right`\n270: \n271: ### Navigation Keys\n272: `home`, `end`, `pageup`, `pagedown`\n273: \n274: ### Function Keys\n275: `f1` through `f12`\n276: \n277: ### Numpad Keys\n278: `numpad0` through `numpad9`, `numpad_add`, `numpad_subtract`, `numpad_multiply`, `numpad_divide`, `numpad_decimal`, `numpad_enter`\n279: \n280: ---\n281: \n282: ## Platform-Specific Bindings\n283: \n284: Use the `platform` field to create platform-specific bindings:\n285: \n286: ```yaml\n287: bindings:\n288:   # macOS only\n289:   - key: \"meta+left\"\n290:     command: MoveCursorLineStart\n291:     platform: macos\n292: \n293:   # Windows/Linux only\n294:   - key: \"ctrl+home\"\n295:     command: MoveCursorDocumentStart\n296:     platform: windows\n297: ```\n298: \n299: Valid platform values: `macos`, `windows`, `linux`",

themes:\n10: \n11: | Theme         | File               | Description                       |\n12: |---------------|--------------------|------------------------------------|\n13: | Default Dark  | `dark.yaml`        | VS Code-inspired dark theme        |\n14: | Fleet Dark    | `fleet-dark.yaml`  | JetBrains Fleet-inspired dark theme|\n15: | GitHub Dark   | `github-dark.yaml` | GitHub's dark theme                |\n16: | GitHub Light  | `github-light.yaml`| GitHub's light theme               |\n17: \n18: ---\n19: \n20: ## Configuration\n21: \n22: ### Theme Location\n23: \n24: - **Built-in themes**: Embedded at compile time from `themes/` directory\n25: - **User themes**: `~/.config/token-editor/themes/` (create directory to add custom themes)\n26: \n27: ### Switching Themes\n28: \n29: Use the Command Palette (Cmd+Shift+A) and search for \"Switch Theme\" to change themes.\n30: \n31: ---\n32: \n33: ## Theme Format\n34: \n35: Themes use YAML with a structured format:\n36: \n37: ```yaml\n38: version: 1\n39: name: \"My Custom Theme\"\n40: author: \"Your Name\"\n41: description: \"A brief description of the theme\"\n42: \n43: ui:\n44:   editor:\n45:     background: \"#1E1E1E\"\n46:     foreground: \"#D4D4D4\"\n47:     # ... more colors\n48: \n49:   syntax:\n50:     keyword: \"#C586C0\"\n51:     function: \"#DCDCAA\"\n52:     # ... more colors\n53: ```\n54: \n55: ### Color Format\n56: \n57: Colors use hexadecimal format:\n58: - **6-digit**: `#RRGGBB` (e.g., `#1E1E1E`)\n59: - **8-digit**: `#RRGGBBAA` with alpha channel (e.g., `#FFFFFF80` for 50% opacity)\n60: \n61: ---\n62: \n63: ## Color Reference\n64: \n65: ### UI Colors\n66: \n67: #### `editor` - Main Editor Area\n68: \n69: | Key                      | Description                          |\n70: |--------------------------|--------------------------------------|\n71: | `background`             | Editor background color              |\n72: | `foreground`             | Default text color                   |\n73: | `current_line_background`| Background of the current line       |\n74: | `cursor_color`           | Primary cursor color                 |\n75: | `selection_background`   | Selected text background             |\n76: | `secondary_cursor_color` | Multi-cursor secondary cursor color  |\n77: \n78: #### `gutter` - Line Numbers Area\n79: \n80: | Key                | Description                           |\n81: |--------------------|---------------------------------------|\n82: | `background`       | Gutter background color               |\n83: | `foreground`       | Line number color (inactive lines)    |\n84: | `foreground_active`| Line number color (current line)      |\n85: | `border_color`     | Border between gutter and editor      |\n86: \n87: #### `status_bar` - Bottom Status Bar\n88: \n89: | Key          | Description              |\n90: |--------------|--------------------------|\n91: | `background` | Status bar background    |\n92: | `foreground` | Status bar text color    |\n93: \n94: #### `sidebar` - File Tree Sidebar\n95: \n96: | Key                    | Description                          |\n97: |------------------------|--------------------------------------|\n98: | `background`           | Sidebar background                   |\n99: | `foreground`           | Default text color                   |\n100: | `selection_background` | Selected item background             |\n101: | `selection_foreground` | Selected item text color             |\n102: | `hover_background`     | Hovered item background              |\n103: | `folder_icon`          | Folder icon color                    |\n104: | `file_icon`            | File icon color                      |\n105: | `border`               | Border between sidebar and editor    |\n106: \n107: #### `tab_bar` - Tab Bar Area\n108: \n109: | Key                  | Description                    |\n110: |----------------------|--------------------------------|\n111: | `background`         | Tab bar background             |\n112: | `active_background`  | Active tab background          |\n113: | `active_foreground`  | Active tab text color          |\n114: | `inactive_background`| Inactive tab background        |\n115: | `inactive_foreground`| Inactive tab text color        |\n116: | `border`             | Tab bar border                 |\n117: | `modified_indicator` | Unsaved changes indicator color|\n118: \n119: #### `overlay` - Dialogs and Command Palette\n120: \n121: | Key Border around selected cell       |\n141: | `number_foreground`       | Color for numeric cell values     |\n142: \n143: ### Syntax Colors\n144: \n145: These colors are used for syntax highlighting |-------------------|------------------------------------|-----------------------|\n149: | `keyword`         | Language keywords                  | `if`, `else`, `fn`    |\n150: | `function`        |   "content": "1: # Implementer's Guide to Modern Text Editor UI Geometry, Mathematics, Edge Cases, and Problems\n2: \n3: A comprehensive reference for building scrollable text editing interfaces with tabs, split panels, soft wrapping,\n4: autocomplete overlays, and all the coordinate transformations in between.\n5: \n6: > **Target Audience:** Desktop software developers building code editors in Rust, Go, C++, or similar languages.\n7: > This guide assumes monospace fonts and left-to-right text direction. Web-specific concerns (DOM, CSS) are\n8: > mentioned only where relevant for conceptual understanding.\n9: \n10: ---\n11: \n12: ## Table of Contents\n13: \n14: 1. [Foundational Concepts: The Scrollable Region](#chapter-1-foundational-concepts-the-scrollable-region)\n15: 2. [Structural Hierarchy: Tabs, Splits, and Editor Groups](#chapter-2-structural-hierarchy-tabs-splits-and-editor-groups)\n16: 3. [The Gutter System](#chapter-3-the-gutter-system)\n17: 4. [Naming Conventions and Terminology](#chapter-11-naming-conventions-and-terminology)\n25: 12. [Cursor Styles: Pipe, Block, and Underline](#chapter-12-cursor-styles-pipe-block-and-underline)\n26: 13. [Keyboard Mapping and Command Dispatch](#chapter-13-keyboard-mapping-and-command-dispatch)\n27: 14. [Mouse Interaction and Drag Selection](#chapter-14-mouse-interaction-and-drag-selection)\n28: \n29: **Appendices:**\n30: \n31: - [A: Event Flow Example](#appendix-a-event-flow-example)\n32: - [B: Coordinate Transformation Functions](#appendix-b-coordinate-transformation-functions)\n33: - [C: Binary Search Helpers](#appendix-c-binary-search-helpers)\n34: - [D: Useful Constants and Defaults](#appendix-d-useful-constants-and-defaults)\n35: - [E: Unicode and UTF-8 Considerations for Code Editors](#appendix-e-unicode-and-utf-8-considerations-for-code-editors)\n36: - [F: Right-to-Left and Bidirectional Text Theory](#appendix-f-right-to-left-and-bidirectional-text-theory)\n37: \n38: ---\n39: \n40: ## Chapter 1: Foundational Concepts: The Scrollable Region\n41: \n42: ### 1.1 Core Entities\n43: \n44: At the most fundamental level, a scrollable region consists of these conceptual components:\n45: \n46: #### Content (Document / Scrollable Content)\n47: \n48: The full extent of what _could_ be displayed. Has its own coordinate system, often called **content coordinates** or\n49: **document coordinates**. For a text editor, this might be 50,000 pixels tall even though only 800 pixels are visible at\n50: any moment.\n51: \n52: #### Viewport (View / Visible Region)\n53: \n54: The rectangular \"window\" through which you see a portion of the content. Has a fixed size (width × height) and its own\n55: coordinate system where (0,0) is typically the top-left corner of what's currently visible.\n56: \n57: #### Scroll Offset (Scroll Position)\n58: \n59: The translation between content coordinates and viewport coordinates. Typically expressed as `(scrollX, scrollY)` — how\n60: far into the content the viewport's top-left corner is positioned.\n61: \n62: #### Scrollable Extent (Scroll Range / Scroll Bounds)\n63: \n64: The maximum distance the content can scroll. Always clamped to non-negative values:\n65: \n66: ```\n67: scrollableHeight = max(0, contentHeight - viewportHeight)\n68: scrollableWidth  = max(0, contentWidth - viewportWidth)\n69: ```\n70: \n71: When `contentHeight <= viewportHeight`, there is no vertical scroll (scrollableHeight = 0).\n72: \n73: ### 1.2 The Scrollbar Anatomy\n74: \n75: The **scrollbar** is the entire control assembly, typically containing:\n76: \n77: | Part           | Alternative Names                | Purpose                                          |\n78: | -------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |\n79: | **Track**      | Gutter, Trough, Lane             | The full channel the thumb travels along         |\n80: | **Thumb**      | Handle, Knob, Scrubber, Elevator | The draggable indicator of current position      |\n81: | **Buttons**    | Arrows, Steppers                 | Optional increment/decrement buttons at ends     |\n82: | **Page Zones** | Gutter regions                   | Clickable areas above/below thumb for page jumps |\n83: \n84: ### 1.3 Key Mathematical Relationships\n85: \n86: #### Thumb Size (Proportional Scrollbars)\n87: \n88: ```\n89: thumbLength / trackLength = viewportSize / contentSize\n90: ```\n91: \n92: The thumb visually represents what fraction of content is visible.\n93: \n94: **Edge case:** When `contentSize <= viewportSize`, either hide the scrollbar entirely or set `thumbLength = trackLength`\n95: (thumb fills the track, indicating no scrolling possible).\n96: \n97: #### Scroll Position ↔ Thumb Position\n98: \n99: ```\n100: // Only valid when scrollableExtent > 0 and trackLength > thumbLength\n101: scrollOffset / scrollableExtent = thumbPosition / (trackLength - thumbLength)\n102: ```\n103: \n104: **Edge cases to handle:**\n105: \n106: - `scrollableExtent == 0`: No scrolling possible, fix `scrollOffset = 0`, `thumbPosition = 0`\n107: - `trackLength == thumbLength`: Thumb fills track, fix `thumbPosition = 0`\n108: \n109: ```\n110: function scrollOffsetToThumbPosition(scrollOffset, scrollableExtent, trackLength, thumbLength) {\n111:     if (scrollableExtent <= 0 || trackLength <= thumbLength) {\n112:         return 0\n113:     }\n114:     return (scrollOffset / scrollableExtent) * (trackLength - thumbLength)\n115: }\n116: \n117: function thumbPositionToScrollOffset(thumbPosition, scrollableExtent, trackLength, thumbLength) {\n118:     if (scrollableExtent <= 0 || trackLength <= thumbLength) {\n119:         return 0\n120:     }\n121:     return (thumbPosition / (trackLength - thumbLength)) * scrollableExtent\n122: }\n123: ```\n124: \n125: #### Content-to-Viewport Transformation\n126: \n127: ```\n128: viewportY = contentY - scrollOffsetY\n129: contentY  = viewportY + scrollOffsetY\n130: ```\n131: \n132: ### 1.4 Coordinate Systems\n133: \n134: When implementing a scrollable region, you're juggling multiple coordinate systems:\n135: \n136: 1. **Content/Document coordinates** — Position within the full scrollable content (pixels)\n137: 2. **Viewport/Client coordinates** — Position within the visible rectangle (pixels)\n138: 3. **Screen/Absolute coordinates** — Position on the physical display (pixels)\n139: 4. **Scrollbar-local coordinates** — For hit-testing thumb, track, and buttons (pixels)\n140: 5. **Document coordinates** — Position within text (line number, column number)\n141: \n142: ### 1.5 Visual Model\n143: \n144: ```\n145: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐\n146: │            Content (Document)           │\n147: │  ┌───────────────────────────────────┐  │\n148: │  │                                   │  │\n149: │  │     (content above viewport)      │  │\n150: │  │ ┌─────┐\n154: │  │ │         VIEWPORT              │ │  │   │ ▲   │\n155: │  │ │      (visible region)         │ │  │   ├─────┤\n156: │  │ │                               │ │  │   │ ░░░ │◄─ thumb\n157: │  │ │                               │ │  │   │ ░░░ │\n158: │  │ └───────────────────────────────┘ │  │   ├─────┤\n159: │  │     (content below viewport)      │  │   │     │◄─ track\n160: ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n177: │ Window                                                                  │\n178: │ content, independent of how it's displayed:\n207: \n208: ```\n209: Document {\n210:     content: Text               // rope, piece table, gap buffer, etc.\n211:     uri: Identifier             // file path or unique identifier\n212:     language: LanguageId        // for syntax highlighting\n213:     version: Number             // increments on edit\n214:     lineCount: Number\n215: \n216:     // Computed/cached\n217:     lineLengths: Array<Number>  // length of each line in columns\n218:     lineOffsets: Array<Number>  // byte offset where each line starts (UTF-8 bytes)\n219: }\n220: ```\n221: \n222: > **Note on offsets:** `lineOffsets` stores byte positions in the UTF-8 encoded buffer. It is purely the data model.\n227: \n228: #### Editor State / View State\n229: \n230: Per-view state for a document. this.lastFrameTime\n1763:         this.lastFrameTime = now\n1764: \n1765:         // Update animations\n1766:         this.updateAnimations(deltaTime)\n1767: \n1768:         // Render\n1769:         this.render()\n1770:     }\n1771: }\n1772: ```\n1773: \n1774: ### 9.3 Render Order (Back to Front)\n1775: \n1776: 1. **Background** — editor background color\n1777: 2. **Current line highlight** — subtle highlight on cursor's line\n1778: 3. **Selection background** — selected text highlight\n1779: 4. **Search match highlights** — find/replace matches\n1780: 5. **Text content** — the actual characters with syntax highlighting\n1781: 6. **Diagnostic underlines** — error/warning squiggles\n1782: 7. **Cursor(s)** — the text cursor(s)\n1783: 8. **Gutter** — line numbers, fold markers, breakpoints\n1784: 9. **Scrollbars** — vertical and horizontal\n1785: 10. **Overlays** — autocomplete, hover, tooltips\n1786: \n1787: ### 9.4 Rendering Text (Monospace, Immediate Mode)\n1788: \n1789: ```\n1790: function renderText(state, ctx, charWidth, lineHeight) {\n1791:     const visibleRange = computeVisibleLines(\n1792:         state.scrollOffset.y,\n1793:         viewport.height,\n1794:         lineHeight,\n1795:         state.document.lineCount\n1796:     )\n1797: \n1798:     // Ensure tokens are ready for visible lines\n1799:     ensureTokenized(state.tokenCache, visibleRange, state.tokenizer)\n1800: \n1801:     for (let line = visibleRange.firstVisible; line < visibleRange.lastVisibleExclusive; line++) {\n1802:         const y = line * lineHeight - key: \"modifier+key\" or just \"key\"\n20: #     command: CommandName\n21: #\n22: # Use \"cmd\" for the platform command key (Cmd on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere)\n23: \n24: bindings:\n25:   # ===========================================================================\n26:   # File Operations\n27:   # ===========================================================================\n28:   -   "content": "1: //! Command enum representing all executable editor actions\n2: //!\n3: //! Commands are the bridge between keybindings and the message system.\n4: //! Each command maps to one or more `Msg` values for the Elm-style update loop.\n5: \n6: use crate::messages::{\n7:     AppMsg, CsvMsg, Direction, DockMsg, DocumentMsg, EditorMsg, LayoutMsg, Msg, PreviewMsg, UiMsg,\n8:     WorkspaceMsg,\n9: };\n10: use crate::model::editor_area::SplitDirection;\n11: use crate::model::ModalId;\n12: use crate::panel::PanelId;\n13: \n14: /// All executable editor commands that can be bound to keys\n15: ///\n16: /// This enum covers every action that can be triggered via keyboard.\n17: /// Commands are organized by category for clarity.\n18: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\n19: pub enum Command {\n20:     // ========================================================================\n21:     // Cursor Movement (no selection)\n22:     // ========================================================================\n23:     /// Move cursor up one line\n24:     MoveCursorUp,\n25:     /// Move cursor down one line\n26:     MoveCursorDown,\n27:     /// Move cursor left one character\n28:     MoveCursorLeft,\n29:     /// Move cursor right one character\n30:     MoveCursorRight,\n31:     /// Move cursor to start of line\n32:     MoveCursorLineStart,\n33:     /// Move cursor to end of line\n34:     MoveCursorLineEnd,\n35:     /// Move cursor to start of document\n36:     MoveCursorDocumentStart,\n37:     /// Move cursor to end of document\n38:     MoveCursorDocumentEnd,\n39:     /// Move cursor left by word\n40:     MoveCursorWordLeft,\n41:     /// Move cursor right by word\n42:     MoveCursorWordRight,\n43:     /// Move cursor up by page\n44:     PageUp,\n45:     /// Move cursor down by page\n46:     PageDown,\n47: \n48:     // ========================================================================\n49:     // Selection Movement (extend selection)\n50:     // ========================================================================\n51:     /// Move cursor up, extending selection\n52:     MoveCursorUpWithSelection,\n53:     /// Move cursor down, extending selection\n54:     MoveCursorDownWithSelection,\n55:     /// Move cursor left, extending selection\n56:     MoveCursorLeftWithSelection,\n57:     /// Move cursor right, extending selection\n58:     MoveCursorRightWithSelection,\n59:     /// Move to line start, extending selection\n60:     MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection,\n61:     /// Move to line end, extending selection\n62:     MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection,\n63:     /// Move to document start, extending selection\n64:     MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection,\n65:     /// Move to document end, extending selection\n66:     MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection,\n67:     /// Move left by word, extending selection\n68:     MoveCursorWordLeftWithSelection,\n69:     /// Move right by word, extending selection\n70:     MoveCursorWordRightWithSelection,\n71:     /// Page up, extending selection\n72:     PageUpWithSelection,\n73:     /// Page down, extending selection\n74:     PageDownWithSelection,\n75: \n76:     // ========================================================================\n77:     // Selection Commands\n78:     // ========================================================================\n79:     /// Select all text in document\n80:     SelectAll,\n81:     /// Select current word\n82:     SelectWord,\n83:     /// Select current line\n84:     SelectLine,\n85:     /// Clear selection (collapse to cursor)\n86:     ClearSelection,\n87:     /// Expand selection to larger semantic unit\n88:     ExpandSelection,\n89:     /// Shrink selection to smaller semantic unit\n90:     ShrinkSelection,\n91: \n92:     // ========================================================================\n93:     // Multi-Cursor\n94:     // ========================================================================\n95:     /// Add a cursor above the current cursor\n96:     AddCursorAbove,\n97:     /// Add a cursor below the current cursor\n98:     AddCursorBelow,\n99:     /// Collapse to single cursor (remove all secondary cursors)\n100:     CollapseToSingleCursor,\n101:     /// Select next occurrence of current word/selection\n102:     SelectNextOccurrence,\n103:     /// Unselect the last added occurrence\n104:     UnselectOccurrence,\n105: \n106:     // ========================================================================\n107:     // Text Editing\n108:     // ========================================================================\n109:     /// Insert a newline at cursor\n110:     InsertNewline,\n111:     /// Delete character before cursor (backspace)\n112:     DeleteBackward,\n113:     /// Delete character at cursor (delete)\n114:     DeleteForward,\n115:     /// Delete word before cursor\n116:     DeleteWordBackward,\n117:     /// Delete word after cursor\n118:     DeleteWordForward,\n119:     /// Delete entire line\n120:     DeleteLine,\n121:     /// Duplicate current line or selection\n122:     Duplicate,\n123:     /// Indent selected lines\n124:     IndentLines,\n125:     /// Unindent selected lines\n126:     UnindentLines,\n127:     /// Insert a tab character (when no selection)\n128:     InsertTab,\n129: \n130:     // ========================================================================\n131:     // Clipboard\n132:     // ========================================================================\n133:     /// Copy selection to clipboard\n134:     Copy,\n135:     /// Cut selection to clipboard\n136:     Cut,\n137:     /// Paste from clipboard\n138:     Paste,\n139: \n140:     // ========================================================================\n141:     // Undo/Redo\n142:     // ========================================================================\n143:     /// Undo last change\n144:     Undo,\n145:     /// Redo last undone change\n146:     Redo,\n147: \n148:     // ========================================================================\n149:     // File Operations\n150:     // ========================================================================\n151:     /// Save current file\n152:     SaveFile,\n153:     /// Save current file with new name\n154:     SaveFileAs,\n155:     /// Open file dialog\n156:     OpenFile,\n157:     /// Fuzzy file finder - search workspace files (Cmd+Shift+O)\n158:     FuzzyFileFinder,\n159:     /// Create new file\n160:     NewFile,\n161:     /// Quit application\n162:     Quit,\n163: \n164:     // ========================================================================\n165:     // Modals/Dialogs\n166:     // ========================================================================\n167:     /// Toggle command palette\n168:     ToggleCommandPalette,\n169:     /// Toggle goto line dialog\n170:     ToggleGotoLine,\n171:     /// Toggle find/replace dialog\n172:     ToggleFindReplace,\n173: \n174:     // ========================================================================\n175:     // Layout (Tabs/Splits)\n176:     // ========================================================================\n177:     /// Create new tab\n178:     NewTab,\n179:     /// Close current tab\n180: SplitHorizontal,\n187:     /// Split editor vertically (stacked)\n188:     SplitVertical,\n189:     /// Focus next editor group\n190:     FocusNextGroup,\n191:     /// Focus previous editor group\n192:     FocusPrevGroup,\n193:     /// Focus editor group by index (1-4)\n194:     FocusGroup1,\n195:     FocusGroup2,\n196:     FocusGroup3,\n197:     FocusGroup4,\n198: \n199:     // ========================================================================\n200:     // Workspace (Sidebar/File Tree)\n201:     // ========================================================================\n202:     /// Toggle sidebar visibility (legacy, use ToggleFileExplorer)\n203:     ToggleSidebar,\n204:     /// Reveal active file in sidebar\n205:     RevealInSidebar,\n206:     /// Select previous item in file tree\n207:     FileTreeSelectPrevious,\n208:     /// Select next item in file tree\n209:     FileTreeSelectNext,\n210:     /// Open selected file or toggle folder in file tree\n211:     FileTreeOpenOrToggle,\n212:     /// Refresh the file tree from disk\n213:     FileTreeRefresh,\n214: \n215:     // ========================================================================\n216:     // Panels/Docks\n217:     // ========================================================================\n218:     /// Toggle file explorer panel (left dock)\n219:     ToggleFileExplorer,\n220:     /// Toggle terminal panel (bottom dock)\n221:     ToggleTerminal,\n222:     /// Toggle outline panel (right dock)\n223:     ToggleOutline,\n224:     /// Close the currently focused dock\n225:     CloseFocusedDock,\n226: \n227:     // ========================================================================\n228:     // Special\n229:     // ========================================================================\n230:     /// Escape key behavior: collapse multi-cursor, then clear selection\n231:     EscapeSmartClear,\n232:     /// Explicitly unbound - disables a default binding\n233:     Unbound,\n234:     /// Open the log file in the editor\n235:     OpenLogFile,\n236: \n237:     // ========================================================================\n238:     // Markdown Preview\n239:     // ========================================================================\n240:     /// Toggle markdown preview pane\n241:     MarkdownTogglePreview,\n242:     /// Open markdown preview to the side\n243:     MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide,\n244: \n245:     // ========================================================================\n246:     // CSV Mode\n247:     // vec![Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::FocusGroupByIndex(2))],\n405:             FocusGroup3 => vec![Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::FocusGroupByIndex(3))],\n406:             FocusGroup4 => vec![Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::FocusGroupByIndex(4))],\n407: \n408:             // Workspace\n409:             ToggleSidebar => vec![Msg::Dock(DockMsg::FocusOrTogglePanel(\n410:                 PanelId::FILE_EXPLORER,\n411:             ))],\n412:             RevealInSidebar => vec![Msg::Workspace(WorkspaceMsg::RevealActiveFile)],\n413:             FileTreeSelectPrevious => vec![Msg::Workspace(WorkspaceMsg::SelectPrevious)],\n414:             FileTreeSelectNext => vec![Msg::Workspace(WorkspaceMsg::SelectNext)],\n415:             FileTreeOpenOrToggle => vec![Msg::Workspace(WorkspaceMsg::OpenOrToggle)],\n416:             FileTreeRefresh => vec![Msg::Workspace(WorkspaceMsg::Refresh)],\n417: \n418:             // Panels/Docks\n419:             ToggleFileExplorer => vec![Msg::Dock(DockMsg::FocusOrTogglePanel(\n420:                 PanelId::FILE_EXPLORER,\n421:             ))],\n422:             ToggleTerminal => vec![Msg::Dock(DockMsg::FocusOrTogglePanel(PanelId::TERMINAL))],\n423:             ToggleOutline => vec![Msg::Dock(DockMsg::FocusOrTogglePanel(PanelId::OUTLINE))],\n424:             CloseFocusedDock => vec![Msg::Dock(DockMsg::CloseFocusedDock)],\n425: \n426:             // Special - these need context-aware handling\n427:             EscapeSmartClear => {\n428:                 // This is handled specially in the keymap dispatch\n429:                 // because it needs to check model state\n430:                 vec![]\n431:             }\n432:             Unbound => vec![], // Explicitly does nothing\n433:             OpenLogFile => {\n434:                 if let Some(log_path) = crate::config_paths::log_file() {\n435:                     vec![Msg::Layout(LayoutMsg::OpenFileInNewTab(log_path))]\n436:                 } else {\n437:                     vec![]\n438:                 }\n439:             }\n440: \n441:             // Markdown preview\n442:             MarkdownTogglePreview => vec![Msg::Preview(PreviewMsg::Toggle)],\n443:             MarkdownOpenPreviewToSide => vec![Msg::Preview(PreviewMsg::Open)],\n444: \n445: vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveDown)],\n449:             CsvMoveLeft => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveLeft)],\n450:             CsvMoveRight => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::MoveRight)],\n451:             CsvNextCell => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::NextCell)],\n452:             CsvPrevCell => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::PrevCell)],\n453:             CsvFirstCell => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::FirstCell)],\n454:             CsvLastCell => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::LastCell)],\n455:             CsvRowStart => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::RowStart)],\n456:             CsvRowEnd => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::RowEnd)],\n457:             CsvPageUp => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::PageUp)],\n458:             CsvPageDown => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::PageDown)],\n459:             CsvExit => vec![Msg::Csv(CsvMsg::Exit)],\n460:         }\n461:     }\n462: \n463:     /// Check if this command is \"simple\" (doesn't need context)\n464:     ///\n465:     /// Simple commands can be dispatched directly without checking model state.\n466:     /// Complex commands (like Escape behavior) need special handling.\n467:     pub fn is_simple(self) -> bool {\n468:         !matches!(self, Command::EscapeSmartClear)\n469:     }\n470: \n471:     /// Global commands work regardless of focus state (sidebar, CSV editing, etc.)\n472:     ///\n473:     /// These are typically modal toggles and app-level actions that should\n474:     /// always be available.\n475:     pub fn is_global(self) -> bool {\n476:         matches!(\n477:             self,\n478:             Command::ToggleCommandPalette\n479:                 | Command::ToggleGotoLine\n480:                 | Command::ToggleFindReplace\n481:                 | Command::FuzzyFileFinder\n482:                 | Command::ToggleSidebar\n483:                 | Command::ToggleFileExplorer\n484:                 | Command::ToggleTerminal\n485:                 | Command::ToggleOutline\n486:                 | Command::CloseFocusedDock\n487:                 | Command::Quit\n488:                 | Command::SaveFile\n489:                 | Command::NewTab\n490:                 | Command::CloseTab\n491:         )\n492:     }\n493: \n494:     /// Get a display name for this command (for command palette, etc.)\n495:     pub fn display_name(self) -> &'static str {\n496:         use Command::*;\n497: \n498:         match self {\n499:             MoveCursorUp => \"Move Cursor Up\",\n500:             MoveCursorDown => \"Move Cursor Down\",\n501:             MoveCursorLeft => \"Move Cursor Left\",\n502:             MoveCursorRight => \"Move Cursor Right\",\n503:             MoveCursorLineStart => \"Move to Line Start\",\n504:             MoveCursorLineEnd => \"Move to Line End\",\n505:             MoveCursorDocumentStart => \"Move to Document Start\",\n506:             MoveCursorDocumentEnd => \"Move to Document End\",\n507:             MoveCursorWordLeft => \"Move Word Left\",\n508:             MoveCursorWordRight => \"Move Word Embedded: Built-in themes compiled into binary\n9: \n10: use std::path::Path;\n11: \n12: use serde::Deserialize;\n13: \n14: // Embed theme YAML files at compile time\n15: pub const DEFAULT_DARK_YAML: {\n22:     /// Stable identifier for config (e.g. \"default-dark\", \"fleet-dark\")\n23:     pub id: &'static str,\n24:     /// Embedded YAML content\n25:     pub yaml: &'static str,\n26: }\n27: \n28: /// Searches in order:\n78: /// 1. `~/.config/token-editor/themes/{id}.yaml`\n79: /// 2. Embedded builtin themes\n80: pub fn load_theme(id: &str) -> Result<Theme, String> {\n81:     // Try user themes directory\n82:     if let Some(user_dir) = crate::config_paths::themes_dir() {\n83: \n95: /// List all available themes from all sources\n96: ///\n97: /// Returns themes grouped by source, with duplicates resolved by priority:\n98: /// user themes override builtins with the same id.\n99: pub fn list_available_themes() -> Vec<ThemeInfo> {\n100:     let mut themes = Vec::new();\n101:     let mut seen_ids = std::collections::HashSet::new();\n102: \n103:     // Collect user {
```
  "content": "1: # Keymap Configuration Reference\n2: \n3: Configure keyboard shortcuts for Token Editor.\n4: \n5: ---\n6: \n7: ## Configuration File\n8: \n9: User keymaps are stored at:\n10: \n11: | Platform | Path |\n12: |----------|------|\n13: | macOS | `~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml` |\n14: | Linux | `~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml` |\n15: | Windows | `%APPDATA%\\token-editor\\keymap.yaml` |\n16: \n17: Create this file to add or override keybindings.\n18: \n19: ---\n20: \n21: ## File Format\n22: \n23: ```yaml\n24: bindings:\n25:   # Simple binding\n26:   - key: \"cmd+s\"\n27:     command: SaveFile\n28: \n29:   # Binding with context condition\n30:   - key: \"tab\"\n31:     command: IndentLines\n32:     when: [\"has_selection\"]\n33: \n34:   # Platform-specific binding\n35:   - key: \"meta+left\"\n36:     command: MoveCursorLineStart\n37:     platform: macos\n38: \n39:   # Disable a default binding\n40:   - key: \"cmd+d\"\n41:     command: Unbound\n42: ```\n43: \n44: ---\n45: \n46: ## Modifier Keys\n47: \n48: | Modifier | macOS | Windows/Linux |\n49: |----------|-------|---------------|\n50: | `cmd` | Command | Ctrl |\n51: | `ctrl` | Control | Control |\n52: | `shift` | Shift | Shift |\n53: | `alt` | Option | Alt |\n54: | `meta` | Command | Win |\n55: \n56: **Note:** Use `cmd` for the platform \"command\" key. It maps to Command on macOS and Ctrl on Windows/Linux.\n57: \n58: ---\n59: \n60: ## Key Names\n61: \n62: ### Character Keys\n63: \n64: Single characters: `a`, `b`, `c`, `1`, `2`, `3`, etc.\n65: \n66: ### Named Keys\n67: \n68: | Key | Name |\n69: |-----|------|\n70: | Enter/Return | `enter` |\n71: | Escape | `escape` |\n72: | Tab | `tab` |\n73: | Backspace | `backspace` |\n74: | Delete | `delete` |\n75: | Space | `space` |\n76: \n77: ### Arrow Keys\n78: \n79: `up`, `down`, `left`, `right`\n80: \n81: ### Navigation Keys\n82: \n83: `home`, `end`, `pageup`, `pagedown`, `insert`\n84: \n85: ### Function Keys\n86: \n87: `f1`, `f2`, `f3`, `f4`, `f5`, `f6`, `f7`, `f8`, `f9`, `f10`, `f11`, `f12`\n88: \n89: ### Numpad Keys\n90: \n91: `numpad0` through `numpad9`, `numpad_add`, `numpad_subtract`, `numpad_multiply`, `numpad_divide`, `numpad_decimal`, `numpad_enter`\n92: \n93: ---\n94: \n95: ## Context Conditions\n96: \n97: Bindings can be conditional using the `when` field:\n98: \n99: | Condition | Description |\n100: |-----------|-------------|\n101: | `has_selection` | One or more cursors have active selection |\n102: | `no_selection` | No cursor has selection |\n103: | `has_multiple_cursors` | More than one cursor active |\n104: | `single_cursor` | Exactly one cursor |\n105: | `in_editor` | Focus is in the editor pane |\n106: \n107: ### Example: Context-Aware Tab\n108: \n109: ```yaml\n110: bindings:\n111:   # Tab with selection: indent\n112:   - key: \"tab\"\n113:     command: IndentLines\n114:     when: [\"has_selection\"]\n115: \n116:   # Tab without selection: insert tab character\n117:   - key: \"tab\"\n118:     command: InsertTab\n119:     when: [\"no_selection\"]\n120: ```\n121: \n122: ---\n123: \n124: ## Platform-Specific Bindings\n125: \n126: Use `platform` to limit a binding to specific operating systems:\n127: \n128: ```yaml\n129: bindings:\n130:   # macOS only: Cmd+Arrow for line navigation\n131:   - key: \"meta+left\"\n132:     command: MoveCursorLineStart\n133:     platform: macos\n134: \n135:   - key: \"meta+right\"\n136:     command: MoveCursorLineEnd\n137:     platform: macos\n138: ```\n139: \n140: Valid platform values: `macos`, `windows`, `linux`\n141: \n142: ---\n143: \n144: ## Disabling Default Bindings\n145: \n146: Use `command: Unbound` to disable a default binding:\n147: \n148: ```yaml\n149: bindings:\n150:   # Disable Cmd+D duplicate\n151:   - key: \"cmd+d\"\n152:     command: Unbound\n153: ```\n154: \n155: ---\n156: \n157: ## Available Commands\n158: \n159: ### File Operations\n160: \n161: | Command | Description |\n162: |---------|-------------|\n163: | `SaveFile` | Save current file |\n164: | `SaveFileAs` | Save with new name |\n165: | `OpenFile` | Open file dialog |\n166: | `OpenFolder` | Open folder dialog |\n167: | `NewTab` | Create new tab |\n168: | `CloseTab` | Close current tab |\n169: \n170: ### Undo/Redo\n171: \n172: | Command | Description |\n173: |---------|-------------|\n174: | `Undo` | Undo last edit |\n175: | `Redo` | Redo undone edit |\n176: \n177: ### Clipboard\n178: \n179: | Command | Description |\n180: |---------|-------------|\n181: | `Copy` | Copy selection |\n182: | `Cut` | Cut selection |\n183: | `Paste` | Paste from clipboard |\n184: \n185: ### Selection\n186: \n187: | Command | Description |\n188: |---------|-------------|\n189: | `SelectAll` | Select entire document |\n190: | `Duplicate` | Duplicate selection or line |\n191: | `SelectNextOccurrence` | Add cursor at next match |\n192: | `UnselectOccurrence` | Remove last added cursor |\n193: | `ExpandSelection` | Expand to word/line/all |\n194: | `ShrinkSelection` | Shrink to previous scope |\n195: | `ClearSelection` | Clear all selections |\n196: | `CollapseToSingleCursor` | Remove all but primary cursor |\n197: \n198: ### Navigation\n199: \n200: | Command | Description |\n201: |---------|-------------|\n202: | `MoveCursorUp` | Move cursor up one line |\n203: | `MoveCursorDown` | Move cursor down one line |\n204: | `MoveCursorLeft` | Move cursor left one character |\n205: | `MoveCursorRight` | Move cursor right one character |\n206: | `MoveCursorLineStart` | Move to start of line |\n207: | `MoveCursorLineEnd` | Move to end of line |\n208: | `MoveCursorWordLeft` | Move to previous word |\n209: | `MoveCursorWordRight` | Move to next word |\n210: | `MoveCursorDocumentStart` | Move to document start |\n211: | `MoveCursorDocumentEnd` | Move to document end |\n212: | `PageUp` | Move up one page |\n213: | `PageDown` | Move down one page |\n214: \n215: ### Navigation with Selection\n216: \n217: All navigation commands have `*WithSelection` variants that extend the selection:\n218: \n219: - `MoveCursorUpWithSelection`\n220: - `MoveCursorDownWithSelection`\n221: - `MoveCursorLeftWithSelection`\n222: - `MoveCursorRightWithSelection`\n223: - `MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection`\n224: - `MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection`\n225: - `MoveCursorWordLeftWithSelection`\n226: - `MoveCursorWordRightWithSelection`\n227: - `MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection`\n228: - `MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection`\n229: - `PageUpWithSelection`\n230: - `PageDownWithSelection`\n231: \n232: ### Editing\n233: \n234: | Command | Description |\n235: |---------|-------------|\n236: | `InsertNewline` | Insert line break |\n237: | `InsertTab` | Insert tab character |\n238: | `DeleteBackward` | Delete character before cursor |\n239: | `DeleteForward` | Delete character after cursor |\n240: | `DeleteWordBackward` | Delete word before cursor |\n241: | `DeleteWordForward` | Delete word after cursor |\n242: | `DeleteLine` | Delete current line |\n243: | `IndentLines` | Indent selected lines |\n244: | `UnindentLines` | Unindent selected lines |\n245: \n246: ### Modals/Dialogs\n247: \n248: | Command | Description |\n249: |---------|-------------|\n250: | `ToggleCommandPalette` | Open/close command palette |\n251: | `ToggleGotoLine` | Open/close go to line |\n252: | `ToggleFindReplace` | Open/close find/replace |\n253: \n254: ### Layout\n255: \n256: | Command | Description |\n257: |---------|-------------|\n258: | `SplitHorizontal` | Split pane horizontally |\n259: | `SplitVertical` | Split pane vertically |\n260: | `NextTab` | Switch to next tab |\n261: | `PrevTab` | Switch to previous tab |\n262: | `FocusNextGroup` | Focus next editor group |\n263: | `FocusPrevGroup` | Focus previous editor group |\n264: | `FocusGroup1` through `FocusGroup4` | Focus specific group |\n265: \n266: ### Workspace\n267: \n268: | Command | Description |\n269: |---------|-------------|\n270: | `ToggleSidebar` | Show/hide sidebar |\n271: | `RevealInSidebar` | Show current file in tree |\n272: \n273: ### Special\n274: \n275: | Command | Description |\n276: |---------|-------------|\n277: | `EscapeSmartClear` | Smart escape cascade |\n278: | `Unbound` | Disable a binding |\n279: \n280: ---\n281: \n282: ## Default Keybindings\n283: \n284: ### File Operations\n285: \n286: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n287: |--------|-----|---------------|\n288: | Save | Cmd+S | Ctrl+S |\n289: | Save As | Cmd+Shift+S | Ctrl+Shift+S |\n290: | Open File | Cmd+O | Ctrl+O |\n291: | Open Folder | Cmd+Shift+O | Ctrl+Shift+O |\n292: | New Tab | Cmd+Shift+N | Ctrl+Shift+N |\n293: | Close Tab | Cmd+W | Ctrl+W |\n294: \n295: ### Editing\n296: \n297: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n298: |--------|-----|---------------|\n299: | Undo | Cmd+Z | Ctrl+Z |\n300: | Redo | Cmd+Shift+Z | Ctrl+Shift+Z |\n301: | Copy | Cmd+C | Ctrl+C |\n302: | Cut | Cmd+X | Ctrl+X |\n303: | Paste | Cmd+V | Ctrl+V |\n304: | Select All | Cmd+A | Ctrl+A |\n305: | Duplicate | Cmd+D | Ctrl+D |\n306: | Delete Line | Cmd+Backspace | Ctrl+Backspace |\n307: \n308: ### Navigation\n309: \n310: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n311: |--------|-----|---------------|\n312: | Line Start | Cmd+Left or Home | Home |\n313: | Line End | Cmd+Right or End | End |\n314: | Word Left | Option+Left | Alt+Left |\n315: | Word Right | Option+Right | Alt+Right |\n316: | Document Start | Ctrl+Home | Ctrl+Home |\n317: | Document End | Ctrl+End | Ctrl+End |\n318: \n319: ### Selection\n320: \n321: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n322: |--------|-----|---------------|\n323: | Expand Selection | Option+Up | Alt+Up |\n324: | Shrink Selection | Option+Down | Alt+Down |\n325: | Select Next Occurrence | Cmd+J | Ctrl+J |\n326: \n327: ### Dialogs\n328: \n329: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n330: |--------|-----|---------------|\n331: | Command Palette | Cmd+Shift+A | Ctrl+Shift+A |\n332: | Go to Line | Cmd+L | Ctrl+L |\n333: | Find/Replace | Cmd+F | Ctrl+F |\n334: \n335: ### Layout\n336: \n337: | Action | Mac | Windows/Linux |\n338: |--------|-----|---------------|\n339: | Toggle Sidebar | Cmd+1 | Ctrl+1 |\n340: | Split Horizontal | Cmd+Shift+Alt+H | Ctrl+Shift+Alt+H |\n341: | Split Vertical | Cmd+Shift+Alt+V | Ctrl+Shift+Alt+V |\n342: | Next Tab | Cmd+Alt+Right | Ctrl+Alt+Right |\n343: | Previous Tab | Cmd+Alt+Left | Ctrl+Alt+Left |\n344: \n345: ---\n346: \n347: ## Binding Precedence\n348: \n349: When multiple bindings match a keystroke:\n350: \n351: 1. **Context conditions** are evaluated (most specific wins)\n352: 2. **Platform-specific** bindings take precedence on matching platforms\n353: 3. **User bindings** override default bindings with same key + conditions\n354: 4. **Later bindings** in the file override earlier ones with same key\n355: \n356: ---\n357: \n358: ## Example: Custom Configuration\n359: \n360: ```yaml\n361: # ~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml\n362: bindings:\n363:   # Remap Cmd+P to command palette (VS Code style)\n364:   - key: \"cmd+p\"\n365:     command: ToggleCommandPalette\n366: \n367:   # Remap Cmd+Shift+P to quick open\n368:   - key: \"cmd+shift+p\"\n369:     command: QuickOpen\n370: \n371:   # Disable duplicate (I never use it)\n372:   - key: \"cmd+d\"\n373:     command: Unbound\n374: \n375:   # Custom split shortcuts\n376:   - key: \"cmd+\\\\\"\n377:     command: SplitVertical\n378: \n379:   - key: \"cmd+shift+\\\\\"\n380:     command: SplitHorizontal\n381: \n382:   # Vim-style j/k navigation in command palette\n383:   - key: \"ctrl+j\"\n384:     command: PaletteNext\n385:     when: [\"in_palette\"]\n386: \n387:   - key: \"ctrl+k\"\n388:     command: PalettePrev\n389:     when: [\"in_palette\"]\n390: ```\n391: \n392: ---\n393: \n394: ## Troubleshooting\n395: \n396: ### Binding Not Working\n397: \n398: 1. Check the key name is correct (see [Key Names](#key-names))\n399: 2. Verify modifiers are in order: `ctrl+shift+alt+key`\n400: 3. Check for conflicting bindings with `when` conditions\n401: 4. Ensure the file is valid YAML (use a linter)\n402: \n403: ### Finding Default Bindings\n404: \n405: The default keymap is embedded in the binary. To see all defaults:\n406: \n407: ```bash\n408: # View the source keymap.yaml\n409: cat $(dirname $(which token))/keymap.yaml\n410: \n411: # Or check the repository\n412: # https://github.com/helgesverre/token/blob/main/keymap.yaml\n413: ```\n414: \n415: ### Resetting to Defaults\n416: \n417: Delete your custom keymap file:\n418: \n419: ```bash\n420: rm ~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml\n421: ```",
  "absolutePath": "~/code/token-editor/docs/user/config-keymap.md"
}
```

```
{
  "content": "1: # Theme Configuration Reference\n2: \n3: Customize the appearance of Token Editor with themes.\n4: \n5: ---\n6: \n7: ## Configuration\n8: \n9: ### Theme Selection\n10: \n11: The active theme is saved in your config file:\n12: \n13: | Platform | Path |\n14: |----------|------|\n15: | macOS | `~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml` |\n16: | Linux | `~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml` |\n17: | Windows | `%APPDATA%\\token-editor\\config.yaml` |\n18: \n19: ```yaml\n20: # config.yaml\n21: theme: \"fleet-dark\"\n22: ```\n23: \n24: ### Theme Files\n25: \n26: Custom themes are stored in:\n27: \n28: | Platform | Path |\n29: |----------|------|\n30: | macOS | `~/.config/token-editor/themes/*.yaml` |\n31: | Linux | `~/.config/token-editor/themes/*.yaml` |\n32: | Windows | `%APPDATA%\\token-editor\\themes\\*.yaml` |\n33: \n34: ---\n35: \n36: ## Built-in Themes\n37: \n38: | Theme | ID | Description |\n39: |-------|-----|-------------|\n40: | Default Dark | `default-dark` | VS Code-inspired dark theme |\n41: | Fleet Dark | `fleet-dark` | JetBrains Fleet dark theme |\n42: | GitHub Dark | `github-dark` | GitHub dark theme |\n43: | GitHub Light | `github-light` | GitHub light theme |\n44: \n45: ---\n46: \n47: ## Theme File Format\n48: \n49: ```yaml\n50: version: 1\n51: name: \"My Custom Theme\"\n52: author: \"Your Name\"\n53: description: \"A custom theme description\"\n54: \n55: ui:\n56:   editor:\n57:     background: \"#1E1E1E\"\n58:     foreground: \"#D4D4D4\"\n59:     current_line_background: \"#2A2A2A\"\n60:     cursor_color: \"#FCE146\"\n61:     selection_background: \"#264F78\"\n62:     secondary_cursor_color: \"#FFFFFF80\"\n63: \n64:   gutter:\n65:     background: \"#1E1E1E\"\n66:     foreground: \"#858585\"\n67:     foreground_active: \"#C6C6C6\"\n68:     border_color: \"#313438\"\n69: \n70:   status_bar:\n71:     background: \"#007ACC\"\n72:     foreground: \"#FFFFFF\"\n73: \n74:   sidebar:\n75:     background: \"#252526\"\n76:     foreground: \"#CCCCCC\"\n77:     selection_background: \"#FFFFFF1A\"\n78:     selection_foreground: \"#FFFFFF\"\n79:     hover_background: \"#FFFFFF0D\"\n80:     folder_icon: \"#DCDC8B\"\n81:     file_icon: \"#9CDCFE\"\n82:     border: \"#3C3C3C\"\n83: \n84:   tab_bar:\n85:     background: \"#252526\"\n86:     active_background: \"#1E1E1E\"\n87:     active_foreground: \"#FFFFFF\"\n88:     inactive_background: \"#2D2D2D\"\n89:     inactive_foreground: \"#808080\"\n90:     border: \"#3C3C3C\"\n91:     modified_indicator: \"#FFFFFF\"\n92: \n93:   overlay:\n94:     border: \"#43454A\"\n95:     background: \"#2B2D30\"\n96:     foreground: \"#E0E0E0\"\n97:     input_background: \"#1E1E1E\"\n98:     selection_background: \"#264F78\"\n99:     highlight: \"#80FF80\"\n100:     warning: \"#FFFF80\"\n101:     error: \"#FF8080\"\n102: \n103:   syntax:\n104:     keyword: \"#C586C0\"\n105:     function: \"#DCDCAA\"\n106:     function_builtin: \"#DCDCAA\"\n107:     string: \"#CE9178\"\n108:     number: \"#B5CEA8\"\n109:     comment: \"#6A9955\"\n110:     type: \"#4EC9B0\"\n111:     variable: \"#9CDCFE\"\n112:     variable_builtin: \"#569CD6\"\n113:     property: \"#9CDCFE\"\n114:     operator: \"#D4D4D4\"\n115:     punctuation: \"#D4D4D4\"\n116:     constant: \"#569CD6\"\n117:     tag: \"#569CD6\"\n118:     attribute: \"#9CDCFE\"\n119:     escape: \"#D7BA7D\"\n120:     label: \"#D7BA7D\"\n121:     text: \"#D4D4D4\"\n122:     text_emphasis: \"#D4D4D4\"\n123:     text_strong: \"#D4D4D4\"\n124:     text_title: \"#569CD6\"\n125:     text_uri: \"#3E9CD6\"\n126: ```\n127: \n128: ---\n129: \n130: ## Color Formats\n131: \n132: Colors can be specified in multiple formats:\n133: \n134: | Format | Example | Description |\n135: |--------|---------|-------------|\n136: | Hex (6-digit) | `#FF5500` | RGB in hexadecimal |\n137: | Hex (8-digit) | `#FF550080` | RGBA with alpha |\n138: | Hex (3-digit) | `#F50` | Shorthand RGB |\n139: \n140: ### Alpha Channel\n141: \n142: Use 8-digit hex for transparency:\n143: \n144: ```yaml\n145: selection_background: \"#264F7880\"  # 50% opacity\n146: secondary_cursor_color: \"#FFFFFF40\"  # 25% opacity\n147: ```\n148: \n149: ---\n150: \n151: ## UI Components\n152: \n153: ### Editor\n154: \n155: | Property | Description |\n156: |----------|-------------|\n157: | `background` | Main editor background |\n158: | `foreground` | Default text color |\n159: | `current_line_background` | Highlight for cursor line |\n160: | `cursor_color` | Cursor/caret color |\n161: | `selection_background` | Selected text background |\n162: | `secondary_cursor_color` | Multi-cursor secondary cursors |\n163: \n164: ### Gutter\n165: \n166: | Property | Description |\n167: |----------|-------------|\n168: | `background` | Line number area background |\n169: | `foreground` | Inactive line numbers |\n170: | `foreground_active` | Current line number |\n171: | `border_color` | Right border of gutter |\n172: \n173: ### Status Bar\n174: \n175: | Property | Description |\n176: |----------|-------------|\n177: | `background` | Status bar background |\n178: | `foreground` | Status bar text |\n179: \n180: ### Sidebar\n181: \n182: | Property | Description |\n183: |----------|-------------|\n184: | `background` | File tree background |\n185: | `foreground` | Default text color |\n186: | `selection_background` | Selected item background |\n187: | `selection_foreground` | Selected item text |\n188: | `hover_background` | Hovered item background |\n189: | `folder_icon` | Folder icon color |\n190: | `file_icon` | File icon color |\n191: | `border` | Right border of sidebar |\n192: \n193: ### Tab Bar\n194: \n195: | Property | Description |\n196: |----------|-------------|\n197: | `background` | Tab bar background |\n198: | `active_background` | Active tab background |\n199: | `active_foreground` | Active tab text |\n200: | `inactive_background` | Inactive tab background |\n201: | `inactive_foreground` | Inactive tab text |\n202: | `border` | Tab separator lines |\n203: | `modified_indicator` | Unsaved file indicator dot |\n204: \n205: ### Overlay (Modals/Dialogs)\n206: \n207: | Property | Description |\n208: |----------|-------------|\n209: | `border` | Modal border color |\n210: | `background` | Modal background |\n211: | `foreground` | Modal text |\n212: | `input_background` | Text input background |\n213: | `selection_background` | Selected list item |\n214: | `highlight` | Success/match highlight |\n215: | `warning` | Warning text |\n216: | `error` | Error text |\n217: \n218: ---\n219: \n220: ## Syntax Highlighting\n221: \n222: ### Token Types\n223: \n224: | Token | Description | Example |\n225: |-------|-------------|---------|\n226: | `keyword` | Language keywords | `if`, `else`, `fn`, `let` |\n227: | `function` | Function names | `calculate()` |\n228: | `function_builtin` | Built-in functions | `print()`, `len()` |\n229: | `string` | String literals | `\"hello\"` |\n230: | `number` | Numeric literals | `42`, `3.14` |\n231: | `comment` | Comments | `// comment` |\n232: | `type` | Type names | `String`, `Vec` |\n233: | `variable` | Variable names | `count`, `name` |\n234: | `variable_builtin` | Built-in variables | `self`, `this` |\n235: | `property` | Object properties | `.length` |\n236: | `operator` | Operators | `+`, `-`, `=` |\n237: | `punctuation` | Punctuation | `{`, `}`, `;` |\n238: | `constant` | Constants | `true`, `false`, `null` |\n239: | `tag` | HTML/XML tags | `<div>` |\n240: | `attribute` | Tag attributes | `class=` |\n241: | `escape` | Escape sequences | `\\n`, `\\t` |\n242: | `label` | Labels/symbols | `@decorator` |\n243: \n244: ### Markdown-Specific\n245: \n246: | Token | Description |\n247: |-------|-------------|\n248: | `text` | Default text |\n249: | `text_emphasis` | *Italic text* |\n250: | `text_strong` | **Bold text** |\n251: | `text_title` | # Headings |\n252: | `text_uri` | Links |\n253: \n254: ---\n255: \n256: ## Creating a Custom Theme\n257: \n258: ### Step 1: Create Theme File\n259: \n260: ```bash\n261: mkdir -p ~/.config/token-editor/themes\n262: touch ~/.config/token-editor/themes/my-theme.yaml\n263: ```\n264: \n265: ### Step 2: Copy Base Theme\n266: \n267: Start from a built-in theme:\n268: \n269: ```yaml\n270: version: 1\n271: name: \"My Theme\"\n272: author: \"Your Name\"\n273: description: \"Custom theme based on default-dark\"\n274: \n275: ui:\n276:   editor:\n277:     background: \"#1A1A2E\"\n278:     foreground: \"#EAEAEA\"\n279:     # ... customize as needed\n280: ```\n281: \n282: ### Step 3: Select Theme\n283: \n284: In-app: Use the Theme Picker (Cmd+Shift+T)\n285: \n286: Or edit config directly:\n287: \n288: ```yaml\n289: # ~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml\n290: theme: \"my-theme\"\n291: ```\n292: \n293: ---\n294: \n295: ## Theme Inheritance (Future)\n296: \n297: Currently themes must define all properties. Future versions may support:\n298: \n299: ```yaml\n300: extends: \"default-dark\"\n301: ui:\n302:   editor:\n303:     background: \"#0D1117\"  # Override only what you need\n304: ```\n305: \n306: ---\n307: \n308: ## Tips for Theme Design\n309: \n310: ### Contrast\n311: \n312: Ensure sufficient contrast between:\n313: - `foreground` and `background`\n314: - `selection_background` and selected text\n315: - `cursor_color` and `background`\n316: \n317: Use a contrast checker tool for accessibility.\n318: \n319: ### Consistency\n320: \n321: - Use consistent color families\n322: - Match `editor.background` with `gutter.background`\n323: - Coordinate `sidebar.background` with `tab_bar.background`\n324: \n325: ### Syntax Highlighting\n326: \n327: - Keywords should stand out (often pink/purple)\n328: - Strings in warm colors (orange/red)\n329: - Comments should be subdued (gray/muted)\n330: - Types in distinctive color (cyan/teal)\n331: \n332: ---\n333: \n334: ## Switching Themes\n335: \n336: ### Via Command Palette\n337: \n338: 1. Open Command Palette (Cmd+Shift+A)\n339: 2. Type \"theme\"\n340: 3. Select \"Change Theme\"\n341: 4. Choose from list\n342: \n343: ### Via Keyboard\n344: \n345: Default: Cmd+Shift+T (if configured)\n346: \n347: ---\n348: \n349: ## Troubleshooting\n350: \n351: ### Theme Not Loading\n352: \n353: 1. Check file is valid YAML (use a linter)\n354: 2. Ensure `version: 1` is present\n355: 3. Verify file is in correct directory\n356: 4. Check for typos in color hex codes\n357: \n358: ### Colors Not Applying\n359: \n360: 1. Restart the editor after theme changes\n361: 2. Verify property names match exactly\n362: 3. Check for missing required properties\n363: \n364: ### Reverting to Default\n365: \n366: Delete your config:\n367: \n368: ```bash\n369: rm ~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml\n370: ```\n371: \n372: Or set explicitly:\n373: \n374: ```yaml\n375: # config.yaml\n376: theme: \"default-dark\"\n377: ```",
```

```
{
  "content": "1: //! Centralized configuration paths for token-editor\n2: //!\n3: //! All config files live under:\n4: //! - Unix/macOS: `~/.config/token-editor/`\n5: //! - Windows: `%APPDATA%\\token-editor\\`\n6: //!\n7: //! This module is the single source of truth for config paths.\n8: \n9: use std::{\n10:     env, fs,\n11:     path::{Path, PathBuf},\n12: };\n13: \n14: const APP_DIR: &str = \"token-editor\";\n15: \n16: /// Base config directory for token-editor\n17: ///\n18: /// Unix/macOS:\n19: ///   - If XDG_CONFIG_HOME is set: `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/token-editor`\n20: ///   - Else: `~/.config/token-editor`\n21: ///\n22: /// Windows:\n23: ///   - `%APPDATA%\\token-editor`\n24: pub fn config_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n25:     #[cfg(target_os = \"windows\")]\n26:     {\n27:         env::var(\"APPDATA\")\n28:             .ok()\n29:             .map(|appdata| PathBuf::from(appdata).join(APP_DIR))\n30:     }\n31: \n32:     #[cfg(not(target_os = \"windows\"))]\n33:     {\n34:         env::var_os(\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\")\n35:             .map(PathBuf::from)\n36:             .or_else(|| dirs::home_dir().map(|h| h.join(\".config\")))\n37:             .map(|config| config.join(APP_DIR))\n38:     }\n39: }\n40: \n41: /// `~/.config/token-editor/themes/`\n42: pub fn themes_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n43:     config_dir().map(|dir| dir.join(\"themes\"))\n44: }\n45: \n46: /// `~/.config/token-editor/config.yaml`\n47: pub fn config_file() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n48:     config_dir().map(|dir| dir.join(\"config.yaml\"))\n49: }\n50: \n51: /// `~/.config/token-editor/keymap.yaml`\n52: pub fn keymap_file() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n53:     config_dir().map(|dir| dir.join(\"keymap.yaml\"))\n54: }\n55: \n56: /// `~/.config/token-editor/logs/`\n57: pub fn logs_dir() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n58:     config_dir().map(|dir| dir.join(\"logs\"))\n59: }\n60: \n61: /// Returns the most recent log file in `~/.config/token-editor/logs/`\n62: /// (e.g., `token.log.2026-01-07`)\n63: ///\n64: /// The logging system uses daily rotation, creating files like `token.log.YYYY-MM-DD`.\n65: /// This function scans the logs directory and returns the newest file.\n66: pub fn log_file() -> Option<PathBuf> {\n67:     let logs_dir = logs_dir()?;\n68: \n69:     // Try to find the most recent token.log.YYYY-MM-DD file\n70:     let mut log_files: Vec<PathBuf> = fs::read_dir(&logs_dir)\n71:         .ok()?\n72:         .filter_map(|entry| entry.ok())\n73:         .map(|entry| entry.path())\n74:         .filter(|path| {\n75:             path.file_name()\n76:                 .and_then(|n| n.to_str())\n77:                 .map(|n| n.starts_with(\"token.log\"))\n78:                 .unwrap_or(false)\n79:         })\n80:         .collect();\n81: \n82:     // Sort by filename in descending order (newest first)\n83:     // YYYY-MM-DD format sorts naturally\n84:     log_files.sort_by(|a, b| b.cmp(a));\n85: \n86:     // Return the most recent log file, or fallback to token.log if none exist\n87:     log_files\n88:         .into_iter()\n89:         .next()\n90:         .or_else(|| Some(logs_dir.join(\"token.log\")))\n91: }\n92: \n93: fn ensure_dir(path: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {\n94:     fs::create_dir_all(path)\n95:         .map_err(|e| format!(\"Failed to create directory {}: {}\", path.display(), e))\n96: }\n97: \n98: /// Ensure the base config dir exists, returning it\n99: pub fn ensure_config_dir() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {\n100:     let dir = config_dir().ok_or_else(|| \"No config directory available\".to_string())?;\n101:     ensure_dir(&dir)?;\n102:     Ok(dir)\n103: }\n104: \n105: /// Ensure themes dir exists, returning it\n106: pub fn ensure_themes_dir() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {\n107:     let config = ensure_config_dir()?;\n108:     let themes = config.join(\"themes\");\n109:     ensure_dir(&themes)?;\n110:     Ok(themes)\n111: }\n112: \n113: /// Ensure logs dir exists, returning it\n114: pub fn ensure_logs_dir() -> Result<PathBuf, String> {\n115:     let config = ensure_config_dir()?;\n116:     let logs = config.join(\"logs\");\n117:     ensure_dir(&logs)?;\n118:     Ok(logs)\n119: }\n120: \n121: /// Ensure full config structure (config dir + themes)\n122: pub fn ensure_all_config_dirs() {\n123:     match ensure_themes_dir() {\n124:         Ok(themes) => {\n125:             tracing::info!(\n126:                 \"Config directories ready (themes dir: {})\",\n127:                 themes.display()\n128:             );\n129:         }\n130:         Err(e) => {\n131:             tracing::warn!(\"Failed to ensure config directories: {}\", e);\n132:         }\n133:     }\n134: }",
  "absolutePath": "~/code/token-editor/src/config_paths.rs"
}
```

1. **`Cmd+Shift+O` → `OpenFolder`** — fixed to `FuzzyFileFinder` (matches `keymap.yaml`)
2. **`Cmd+1` → `ToggleSidebar`** — fixed to `ToggleFileExplorer` (matches `keymap.yaml`)
3. **Missing bindings**: Added `Cmd+2` → `ToggleTerminal`, `Cmd+7` → `ToggleOutline`, `Cmd+Shift+V` → `MarkdownTogglePreview`
4. **Context conditions**: Added missing `modal_inactive`, `editor_focused`, `sidebar_focused` (from `context.rs`)
5. **macOS shortcuts**: Fixed `Cmd+Left/Right` → `Meta+Left/Right` to match the actual `meta` modifier in `keymap.yaml`

#### docs/user/config-keymap.md
1. **`OpenFolder` command** — replaced with `FuzzyFileFinder` (doesn't exist in `Command` enum)
2. **`in_editor` condition** — replaced with actual conditions from code (`modal_active`, `modal_inactive`, `editor_focused`, `sidebar_focused`)
3. **Default keybindings table**: Fixed `Open Folder` → `Go to File`, `Toggle Sidebar` → `Toggle File Explorer` + added Terminal/Outline
4. **Missing commands**: Added `NewFile`, `Quit`, `OpenLogFile`, all Panel/Dock commands, Markdown Preview commands, File Tree commands
5. **Example config**: Removed references to non-existent `QuickOpen`, `PaletteNext`, `PalettePrev`, `in_palette`

#### docs/user/config-theme.md
1. **3-digit hex `#F50`** — removed; `Color::from_hex()` only supports 6 and 8 digit formats

Just add a `3` arm to the match in `Color::from_hex` that doubles each hex digit (`#F50` → `#FF5500`), and optionally a `4` arm for `#RGBA` shorthand. That's the only code change needed — everything else (YAML parsing, theme loading) already flows through `from_hex`.

```rust
3 => Ok(Color {
    r: u8::from_str_radix(&s[0..1].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    g: u8::from_str_radix(&s[1..2].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    b: u8::from_str_radix(&s[2..3].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    a: 255,
}),
4 => Ok(Color {
    r: u8::from_str_radix(&s[0..1].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    g: u8::from_str_radix(&s[1..2].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    b: u8::from_str_radix(&s[2..3].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
    a: u8::from_str_radix(&s[3..4].repeat(2), 16).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
}),
```

No Electron. No bloat. class=\"features\">\n54:   <h2 class=\"section-title\">Built for speed</h2>\n55:   <div class=\"feature-grid\">\n56:     <div class=\"feature-card wide\">\n57:       <span class=\"feature-num\">01</span>\n58:       <h3>Multi-cursor editing</h3>\n59:       <p>Add cursors anywhere with Option+Click. Overlapping cursors merge automatically.</p>\n60:       <div class=\"feature-demo\">\n61:         <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">12</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"alice\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n62:         <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">13</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"bob\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n63:         <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"ln\">14</span><span class=\"kw\">let</span> <span class=\"var\">name</span> = <span class=\"str\">\"carol\"</span>;<span class=\"cursor-block\">█</span></div>\n64:       </div>\n65:       <div class=\"feature-keys\"><kbd>⌥</kbd>+<kbd>Click</kbd> add cursor &nbsp; <kbd>⌘</kbd>+<kbd>J</kbd> select next match</div>\n66:     </div>\n67:     <div class=\"feature-card\">\n68:       <span class=\"feature-num\">02</span>\n69:       <h3>Split views</h3>\n70:       <p>Horizontal and vertical splits with independent viewports. Not regex. Accurate highlighting.</p>\n81:       <div class=\"feature-demo compact\">\n82:         <div class=\"demo-line\"><span class=\"kw\">fn</span> <span class=\"fn\">parse</span>(<span class=\"var\">input</span>: &amp;<span class=\"typ\">str</span>)</div>\n83:         <div class=\"demo-line\">  -> <span class=\"typ\">Result</span>&lt;<span class=\"typ\">AST</span>, <span class=\"typ\">Error</span>&gt;</div>\n84:       </div>\n85:     </div>\n86:     <div class=\"feature-card\">\n87:       <span class=\"feature-num\">04</span>\n88:       <h3>CSV spreadsheet</h3>\n89:       <p>View CSV and TSV as proper tables.
```
Supported languages are registered and defined in the `src/syntax/` directory, primarily using a three-part system: identification, parser mapping, and query definitions.

### 1. Language Identification
The `LanguageId` enum in `src/syntax/languages.rs` defines all supported languages. It includes logic for detecting languages from file extensions, special filenames (like `Makefile` or `Cargo.lock`), and markdown code fences.

- [src/syntax/languages.rs#L9-L39](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/languages.rs#L9-L39) — `LanguageId` enum definition.
- [src/syntax/languages.rs#L43-L75](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/languages.rs#L43-L75) — Extension-to-language mapping.
- [src/syntax/languages.rs#L78-L99](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/languages.rs#L78-L99) — Filename-based detection.

### 2. Tree-Sitter Parser & Query Mapping
The `ParserState` in `src/syntax/parser.rs` manages the actual tree-sitter instances and maps each `LanguageId` to its respective tree-sitter grammar crate and highlight query.

- [src/syntax/parser.rs#L94-L126](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L94-L126) — Embedding of `.scm` query files (via `include_str!`) and references to built-in crate queries.
- [src/syntax/parser.rs#L217-L257](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/parser.rs#L217-L257) — `init_language` method mapping IDs to `tree_sitter_X::language()`.

### 3. Highlight Queries
Custom highlight queries are stored as Scheme files in the root `queries/` directory, while others use the default queries provided by the tree-sitter grammar crates.

- [queries/](file://~/code/token-editor/queries/) — Directory containing custom `.scm` highlight definitions.
- [src/syntax/highlights.rs](file://~/code/token-editor/src/syntax/highlights.rs) — Maps tree-sitter capture names (e.g., `@function.method`) to internal theme colors.
```

Now I have a complete picture. Here's my analysis of all inaccuracies found:

**`website/src/data/keybindings.ts`:**

1. **Line 26**: Action says "Open Folder" but command is `FuzzyFileFinder` — label is misleading, should be "Go to File"
2. **Lines 56-58**: `AddCursor`, `AddCursorAbove`, `AddCursorBelow` with keys `⌥+Click`, `⌥⌥↑`, `⌥⌥↓` — the double-tap Option shortcuts (`⌥⌥↑`/`⌥⌥↓`) don't exist in `keymap.yaml`; `AddCursorAbove`/`AddCursorBelow` have no default binding

**`website/src/pages/index.astro`:**

4. **Line 188**: Says "Open folder" for `⌘⇧O` — should be "Go to File" (it's `FuzzyFileFinder`)

6. **Lines 201**: `⌥⌥↓` "Cursor below" — this double-tap-Option binding doesn't exist in `keymap.yaml`
7. **Line 43**: Claims "20+ Languages" — the `LanguageId` enum has 19 entries (counting PlainText, which isn't really a "language") — actual language count is 18

**`website/src/data/themes.ts` — Default Dark colors are wrong:**
